FUNCTION: llm_write_formula_from_subsets
Asking LLM to write formula from qualifying subsets using Prompt: 
 
    # ROLE: You are expert in Natural language inference.
    # TASK: Given the propositions:
    
      - P_10: "Charlie Francis stayed at Lynda Huey's home in Los Angeles in 1980"
  - P_12: "Lynda Huey said she had seen Charlie Francis on a practice track at Seoul"
  - P_13: "Charlie Francis greeted Lynda Huey as an old friend"
  - P_9: "Lynda Huey said she had known Charlie Francis since 1980"

    
    The following sets 
    
      - {P_9, P_12} (score: 0.70)
  - {P_13, P_12, P_10} (score: 0.70)

    
    are believed to imply the following hypothesis: 
    
    "Because she hosted him in 1980, Huey recognized Francis in Seoul and he greeted her warmly.". 
    
    Here, the score is between 0 and 1 and it reflects our confidence on the implication, with 1 being the higher. 
    
    Your task is to write a propositional logic formula equivalent to the hypothesis by following the next rule.

    # RULES:
    - Use ONLY above proposition IDs
    - Use & for AND, | for OR, ~ for NOT
    - Think carefully about the proposition and hypothesis.
    - Prefer simpler formulas (if one subset is sufficient, use just that)
    - If you find the formula, output ONLY the formula, nothing else
    - If you cannot find the formula, output only "NONE"

    #OUTPUT
